# Agents · S · part 26

977 entries, sf-fix to shell-runner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sf-fix**: SaaSFoundry fix tier: claude-opus-5-5 at medium effort. Use for bug-fix (diagnosing and fixing a defect), review (reviewing a diff or a pull request). Do not use for exploration, prose (use sf-light); scoped-edit (use sf-edit); multi-file-feature, tests (use sf-build); planning (use sf-plan); terminal-ci (use sf-terminal). ([DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI](https://github.com/DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-fix.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **sf-flow-agent**: Build, test, and review Record-Triggered, Screen, Scheduled, and Orchestration Flows with approvals and sub-flow decomposition. Use PROACTIVELY when modifying Flows. For new features, use sf-architect first. Do NOT use for Apex or LWC. ([jiten-singh-shahi/salesforce-claude-code](https://github.com/jiten-singh-shahi/salesforce-claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-flow-agent.md) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **sf-impl-reviewer**: Reviews implementation against specification for quality, compliance, and best practices ([ivkan/specflow-cc](https://github.com/ivkan/specflow-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/impl-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **sf-implementer**: SwarmFusion implementer. Use for executing a plan step or making precise code changes. Writes complete, production-quality code with no placeholders. ([andrewr303/claude-codex-plugin-lab](https://github.com/andrewr303/claude-codex-plugin-lab/blob/HEAD/plugins/swarmfusion/agents/sf-implementer.md))
- **sf-implementer**: Software Factory default implementer pin (grok-4.6). Documents the canonical implementer. Do not spawn this as a nested worker for ordinary work already running on grok-4.6. Do not launch a planner unless a human already selected exactly one planner after a listed escalation criterion. ([Bthornton1994/Virtual-Assistant](https://github.com/Bthornton1994/Virtual-Assistant/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/sf-implementer.md))
- **sf-init-project-skill**: Inspect the current Salesforce project metadata and build or refresh the local indexed Knowledge folder. ([lucabenedettini/salesforce-ai-agent-optimizer](https://github.com/lucabenedettini/salesforce-ai-agent-optimizer/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-init-project-skill.md) · ★1)
- **sf-intake**: First step of /sf-ticket. Reads a Salesforce request (Jira key, Slack thread, or pasted text) and writes intake.md with lane, support type and outcome, falsifiable claims, scope, acceptance criteria, missing info, and prior art from Jira and Confluence. Read-only; never writes to Jira, Confluence or Slack. ([beck-systems/salesforce-agentic-sdlc](https://github.com/beck-systems/salesforce-agentic-sdlc/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-intake.md) · ★1)
- **sf-integration-agent**: Build and review Salesforce integrations — REST/SOAP callouts, Named Credentials, Platform Events, CDC, retry via Finalizers. Use PROACTIVELY when building integrations. For new features, use sf-architect first. Do NOT use for internal Apex or LWC. ([jiten-singh-shahi/salesforce-claude-code](https://github.com/jiten-singh-shahi/salesforce-claude-code/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/sf-integration-agent.md) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **sf-integration-engineer**: Implements outbound and inbound Salesforce integration metadata and integration Apex - Named and External Credentials, External Services, platform event channels, callout services, and event publishers. - **sf-naming**: You are the **Naming Conventions** subagent for a Salesforce org audit. - **sf-qa**: Independent QA for a Salesforce change before it ships. - **shared-dify-https-fallback**: 第二环境使用既有同域名、精确来源受限的正式服务API入口，现行决定见[ADR0306](../adr/0306-formal-scoped-dify-service-ingress.md)。 原[ADR0266](../adr/0266-temporary-shared-dify-https.md)保留临时恢复的历史依据；本次用途转正不改变配置或暴露范围。 部署目标、来源IP、凭据和原始网络证据留在私有记录。公网可达与真实应用验证完成前不得写成恢复成功。 ([kilbertert/AI-Ops](https://github.com/kilbertert/AI-Ops/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/shared-dify-https-fallback.md))
- **shared-document-reviewer**: Use when reviewing documents for consistency and completeness. Reviews document consistency and completeness, providing approval decisions. ([Josh-S-N2M/feature-pipeline](https://github.com/Josh-S-N2M/feature-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shared-document-reviewer.md))
- **shared-engineer**: Apply-time engineer for changes whose primary paths live in shared/. Implements, tests, debugs under the iron-law TDD / verification stack. Dispatched by openspec-apply-change per-task and by superpowers-subagent-driven-development as the implementer agent in place of general-purpose. Enforces the dependency-arrow rule (plugins import shared/, not vice versa), the stdlib-plus-approved-deps-only rule, and the breaking-change-is-BREAKING discipline. ([benni-arnarsson/ormstunga](https://github.com/benni-arnarsson/ormstunga/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/shared-engineer.md))
- **shared-error-aligner**: Следит за исключениями, error messages и единым способом обработки ошибок, когда в коде расползаются разные стили и обработчики ([SergeuMatyasov/backend-agent-kit](https://github.com/SergeuMatyasov/backend-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/shared-error-aligner.agent.md))

- **shared-error-handling**: If any tool or API call you depend on to complete your mandated action fails (e.g. a `gh api` call returns non-zero / 403, a file edit or push fails), you MUST NOT fail silently or surface the problem only in your transcript. - **shared-module-owner**: Pemilik fondasi bersama proyek: skema basis data/migrasi, seed reference data, model, dan kode atau tipe lintas modul (envelope respons, kode error, klaim auth, helper peran). Pada proyek satu modul, perannya skema dan migrasi. Delegasikan setiap pekerjaan yang menyentuh skema atau kode yang dipakai lebih dari satu modul; jalankan sebelum implementer modul mulai. - **shared-skills**: - 적용일: 2026-10-05 - 대상: Codex, Claude Code, Antigravity, Hermes `edith`만 - 이 문서는 공유 스킬의 선택·제외·재설치 기준을 소유한다. - 외부 스킬의 설치 출처·경로·폴더 해시·갱신 시각은 [설치 기록](../../macos/agents/.agents/.skill-lock.json)이 소유한다. - 원본 위치·에이전트 연결·배포 도구를 전환할 때는 [전역 스킬·MCP 관리 방안](global-management.md)을 따른다. 직접 제작 Zotero 원본은 dotfiles로 전환했고 DEVONthink는 볼트 로컬로 유지한다. 스킬 배포 자동화는 후속 범위다. - **shared-slice-planner**: Берет большую задачу и разбивает ее на несколько независимых change slices или PR-этапов, когда она слишком большая для одного change set ([SergeuMatyasov/backend-agent-kit](https://github.com/SergeuMatyasov/backend-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/shared-slice-planner.agent.md))
- **shared-solid-guard**: Следит за соблюдением SOLID в новом и затронутом legacy-коде: SRP, OCP, LSP, ISP, DIP, избыточные абстракции и safe follow-up на найденные нарушения ([SergeuMatyasov/backend-agent-kit](https://github.com/SergeuMatyasov/backend-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/shared-solid-guard.agent.md))
- **shared-standup**: Use when generating standup notes, checking what tasks are in progress, or preparing a sync update for the team. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shared-standup.md))
- **shared-stash-stack**: **A linked worktree isolates the working tree. It does not isolate refs.** A stash entry lives at `refs/stash`, and `refs/stash` lives in the repository's *common* git dir - the one every worktree shares. So every worktree of a repository, plus the main checkout, pushes onto and pops off **one stack**. ([cooneycw/claude-power-pack](https://github.com/cooneycw/claude-power-pack/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/shared-stash-stack.md) · ★21)
- **shared-state-auditor**: Use this agent to find global/static shared mutable state in C extension code that becomes unsafe under free-threaded Python.
